AZURV1 photon benchmark: MC/DC vs. Ganapol analytical solution
====================================================================================================
Analytical model: infinite medium, isotropic planar pulse S(x,t)=delta(x)delta(t), c = 0.5 (ConstantCrossSectionMaterial, sigma_scatter=0.5, sigma_absorb=0.5, sigma_total=1.0)
Reference: Ganapol (2001), LA-UR-01-1854; restated in Bennett & McClarren (2022), arXiv:2205.15783
Tally time grid stored in native units (ns); converted to mean-free-time via t_mft = t_ns * 29.9792458 for comparison against the analytical solution.
APPLY_DX_CORRECTION: True (divides raw MC/DC flux by the x-bin width dx -- confirmed necessary; MC/DC's tracklength_tally scores raw distance*weight with no volume normalization built in; see script header for verification against the 1e9-particle run).
Points within |x/t - 1| < 1e-6 of the causal wavefront are excluded (NaN) -- the exact solution is singular there.
Analytical reference is BIN-AVERAGED (Q_SUB=7x7 Gauss-Legendre sub-quadrature per cell), matching what MC/DC's tracklength tally estimates -- not evaluated at the bin midpoint.
Summary statistics (Plot 1, RMS/max/mean_sdev columns below) exclude time bins below t=2.0 mft: Ganapol's uncollided term e^(-t)/(2t) diverges as t->0, making the true bin-average analytically undefined near the pulse origin in time (verified: mean|err|/sdev = 977 at t=0.5 mft vs. the expected sqrt(2/pi)=0.798 for an unbiased estimator, settling to 0.75-1.05 from t=5.5 mft onward). The full per-run error table further below still includes every time bin.
====================================================================================================
